# Guanzhi 官制 - 列侯册封效果. root=受封者; 纯 trait+旗标, 不建头衔
# 显示: 有地者经整合包风味值覆盖挂载, 无头衔者经 gzfv_titleless 风味条目挂载

# 册封河内王
guanzhi_jue_grant_wang_0000 = {
	guanzhi_clear_jue_effect = yes
	scope:recipient = {
		add_trait = gzj_wang_0000
		# 有地角色不打风味旗标(防压整合包风味)
		if = {
			limit = { is_landed = no }
			add_character_flag = guanzhi_jue_wang_0000
		}
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_jueholder
		guanzhi_fix_titleless_nickname_effect = yes
	}
	debug_log = "GUANZHI jue granted"
}

	guanzhi_clear_jue_effect = yes
	scope:recipient = {
		add_trait = gzj_xian_0209
		# 有地角色不打风味旗标(防压整合包风味)
		if = {
			limit = { is_landed = no }
			add_character_flag = guanzhi_jue_xian_0209
		}
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_jueholder
		guanzhi_fix_titleless_nickname_effect = yes
	}
	debug_log = "GUANZHI jue granted"
}

	guanzhi_clear_jue_effect = yes
	scope:recipient = {
		add_trait = gzj_xian_0322
		# 有地角色不打风味旗标(防压整合包风味)
		if = {
			limit = { is_landed = no }
			add_character_flag = guanzhi_jue_xian_0322
		}
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_jueholder
		guanzhi_fix_titleless_nickname_effect = yes
	}
	debug_log = "GUANZHI jue granted"
}

	guanzhi_clear_jue_effect = yes
	scope:recipient = {
		add_trait = gzj_xian_0435
		# 有地角色不打风味旗标(防压整合包风味)
		if = {
			limit = { is_landed = no }
			add_character_flag = guanzhi_jue_xian_0435
		}
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_jueholder
		guanzhi_fix_titleless_nickname_effect = yes
	}
	debug_log = "GUANZHI jue granted"
}

	guanzhi_clear_jue_effect = yes
	scope:recipient = {
		add_trait = gzj_xian_0548
		# 有地角色不打风味旗标(防压整合包风味)
		if = {
			limit = { is_landed = no }
			add_character_flag = guanzhi_jue_xian_0548
		}
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_jueholder
		guanzhi_fix_titleless_nickname_effect = yes
	}
	debug_log = "GUANZHI jue granted"
}
